# Audit v2 ledger (open claims)

Generated by `python3 scripts/audit/ledger.py` from `status.json` (September 26, 2026). Regenerate after each batch; do not edit by hand.

**3646 open claims on 839 pages.** 108 claims were dropped as settled by sources supplied on September 26 (token-checked against `sources-local/`).

| Severity | Meaning | Open claims |
|---|---|---|
| S1 | dose, contraindication, safety | 162 |
| S2 | guideline grade or wording | 557 |
| S3 | technique, IFU, device detail | 369 |
| S4 | abstract-only or number | 2490 |
| S5 | history, eponym | 68 |

| Disposition | Claims |
|---|---|
| waiting-on-source | 3473 |
| waiting-on-decision | 7 |
| accepted-limitation | 166 |

Severity is keyword-assigned (S1 checked first, then S5, S2, S3, S4); treat it as a triage order, not a clinical judgment. Decision numbers refer to `needed-from-user.md` (section A) and `NEXT-PHASE-PLAN.md`.
